October 026

A Message From The Founder Of PubLife

We're halfway through October, there's a chill in the air, and it's time for another PubLife Monthly!

Over the past month, PubLife has been busy building partnerships, strengthening our operating systems, and continuing our mission to serve the hospitality industry.

Through new relationships with UMKC and Jackson County Public Health, PubLife has joined the Integrated Referral and Intake System (IRIS), developed by the University of Kansas Center for Public Partnerships and Research. Participation connects us with a broader network of organizations and providers, helping us send and receive referrals so hospitality workers can better connect with the resources they need.

We're seeing more applicants and growing support from our community. Thank you to everyone who has helped PubLife reach this point.

If You Missed It...

At the end of September, Heather Graves and two clinicians from Heartland Mental Health joined us at Afterword Tavern & Shelves for our first PubLife Public Forum. Supporters from a range of ages and professional backgrounds took part in an engaged conversation and learned about resources and tools for navigating mental health inside and outside the workplace.

Heartland Mental Health graciously offered hospitality workers in our community access to discounted services starting as low as $60 per hour. Thank you, Heather, for sharing your expertise and for being the first speaker at a PubLife forum.

We also thank Afterword for donating its Emerald City room, which is ideal for gatherings of 10â€“50 people, and Whole Harvest Kitchen for providing a wonderful plant-based spread for our guests.

We're planning more events for our supporters and donors. Keep an eye out for holiday announcements and a possible PubLife Presents Pop-Up coming later this month.

Save the Date:


Chicken N Pickle Giveback Night

Tuesday, November 24, 2026 | 6â€“8 PM

Chicken N Pickle â€¢ 1761 Burlington St,


North Kansas City, MO 64116

Join us for pre-Thanksgiving pickleball, bags, and more! PubLife will have a resource table and free materials, with a chance to pick up some PubLife swag. Ten percent of food and beverage sales will go directly to PubLife to help hospitality workers in our area cover unexpected medical expenses.

For upcoming events, follow us on Facebook and Instagram or email events@publife.org.

Partner Spotlight

Heartland Mental Health

Hope, healing, and support without shame

This month, PubLife is proud to spotlight Heartland Mental Health, founded in 2020 by Heather Graves, M.A., LCPC, LMAC, NPT-C, NCC.

Heather specializes in trauma and addiction, combining professional training with a personal understanding of how these challenges can affect individuals and families across generations. Her work is grounded in a powerful belief: when people feel hope, healing is possible.

Her experience includes crisis intervention, survivor advocacy, substance use recovery, and outpatient addiction treatment. Her practice incorporates therapeutic approaches that help individuals and families navigate difficult experiences and move toward recovery.

We're especially grateful that Heather graciously served as the first speaker at a PubLife forum. Thank you, Heather, for sharing your expertise and supporting our mission to serve the people who serve our community.

Heartland Mental Health offers discounted services for hospitality workers, with rates starting as low as $60 per hour. This can provide people in our industry with a more accessible way to explore professional support.

At PubLife, we believe hospitality workers deserve dignity, compassion, and support both inside and outside the workplace. Thank you, Heather, for being part of the PubLife community and helping others find a path toward hope and healing.

Learn more at heartlandmentalhealth.net

Added Gratuity

As PubLife continues to grow, we're thankful for every applicant who has trusted us during a difficult time.


PubLife was created to help hospitality workers when unexpected medical expenses arise. Thank you for allowing us to be part of your path forward.

Our funding is limited, and we cannot assist everyone financially. As PubLife develops, we hope to help where we can and connect applicants with other useful community resources whenever possible.

To everyone who has trusted us in a time of need: thank you.

Get Involved

PubLife is continuing to build fundraising efforts and programs, and individual donations remain essential. Please consider supporting our Zeffy campaign by setting up a monthly or quarterly contribution. Every amount helps us serve local hospitality workers.



Follow PubLife on Instagram and Facebook, visit publife.org, or donate through Zeffy.


For help with contributions email donate@publife.org.


For less than a cup of coffee per month, you can make al difference for our local hospitality industry!
